Provenance note: Header prepended by the Library thread; the host's front matter up to and including the line beginning 'at |http: //books' removed (47 lines); nothing else altered, no OCR error corrected, nothing retyped. Google Books scan from the New York Public Library; the Google notice at the head of the file is removed as recorded above; the title page is garbled, the journals readable with the long s rendered as f.
Content note: Coke's journals of his five American voyages from 1784, including the Christmas Conference of 1784 that organised the Methodist Episcopal Church and his antislavery preaching in Virginia; the British-American hinge in the superintendent's own record.
